Second transcription's note: Self-employment is read as having a Schedule C on the return, because other self-employment is outside the tool's scope. The statutory employee case is screened per business by sc.statutory. The meaning of s1.has_sc is inferred from its name.

Ruling: scope; i1040gi p. 42, Step 5, question 2: "Were you self-employed at any time in 2025, or are you filing Schedule SE because you were a member of the clergy or you had church employee income, or are you filing Schedule C as a statutory employee? Yes. Skip question 3 and Step 6; go to Worksheet B." The reply reads it as s1.has_sc or its eic.clergy (constant no, refusing on scope.schedule1); the project counts Schedules C (eic.sc_count). executable-triage-v2 (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/eic.json`; 1,382 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 161 partial records; 2,043 comparisons): 2,007 of 2,043 agree; 22 candidate-unknown, 14 candidate-refuses; on returns the full interview completes: none. No completed-return difference. The 22 candidate-unknown (16 incomplete, 6 refused) are records that omit scope.schedule1 (the ss.* fixtures), which the interview asks of every filer; the 14 candidate-refuses are its scope.schedule1 refusal on returns the project refuses on that cited screen. Project stands.

Second transcription's note: Only STOPs that print the line 27c instruction set this box. The other STOPs (for example Step 1 question 1, Step 2, and Step 5 question 3) do not. The election 'if you do not want to claim the earned income credit' has no input in the closed interface and is not represented. The nonresident alien instruction is covered by the scope.other_income refusal.

Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 43, Line 27c: "Check the box on line 27c if you do not want to claim the earned income credit or if you have been instructed to check the box in the instructions for line 27a." i1040gi p. 40, Step 1, question 1: "If, in 2025: 3 or more children who have valid SSNs lived with you, is the amount on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b, less than $61,555 ($68,675 if married filing jointly)? ... No. STOP You can’t take the credit." executable-triage-v2 (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/eic.json`; 1,382 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 161 partial records; 2,043 comparisons): 1,957 of 2,043 agree; 45 candidate-unknown, 40 candidate-refuses, 1 project-refuses; on returns the full interview completes: 13 candidate-unknown. Every completed-return difference was read with the witness cap lifted (triage.mjs run directly; scratch /tmp/adj-fixr2-dependent-eic/eic.full.json): the 13 completed candidate-unknowns are dependent.us_half: the reply's Step 1 limit is chosen by its count of qualifying children with valid SSNs (eic.h_limit through eic.h_kids and dependent.eic_ssn_child), which needs each dependent's dependent.us_half even where line 11b is at or above the largest limit and question 1 is No for any count; the project tests that limit first (eic.below_top) and the interview does not ask those answers there. A candidate that needs answers the source does not need is a model error (README). All 13: stage8.hoh.child.ctc, stage8.single.dependent.boundaries, stage8.single.dependent.students_and_age17, stage8.single.dependent.household_and_ssn, audit.dependent.listed_not_dependent_no_tin and audit.household.hoh_married_no_community_property (line 11b $120,000.00), stage8.single.phaseout.partial and audit.household.single_community_property ($215,300.00); stage13.ctc_spouse_ssn, stage13.odc_spouse_no_tin and stage13.ctc_spouse_no_id (married filing jointly, $120,000.00); stage8.single.taxpayer_without_tin and stage8.single.taxpayer_without_work_ssn ($120,000.00). Off completed returns: 31 more such unknowns, 1 dependent.kidnapped unknown (refused). The candidate-refuses are its gates on refused returns (dependent.other_claimant 20, sa.excluded_income 12, dependent.married 8): scope. The project-refuses is sa.dependent on stage9.eic.refuse.dependent_of_another (cited). No value difference: the reply checks the box only on STOPs that print the instruction, and triage finds it agrees with the project wherever both compute. Project stands.

Second transcription's note: Open: read literally, the No answer to Step 3 question 6 tells every filer reaching it to check this box, including unmarried filers. Chose married taxpayers filing separately or as head of household only, because the Special rule for separated spouses applies only 'If you are married, but not filing a joint return'.

Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 41, Step 3, question 3: "Are you a married taxpayer whose filing status is married filing separately or head of household? Yes. Continue No. Skip questions 4 and 5; go to question 6"; question 6: "No. Check the box in the Dependents section that discusses the special rule for separated spouses" (amb.2025.separated_spouses_box). i1040gi p. 40, Step 1, question 1: "If, in 2025: 3 or more children who have valid SSNs lived with you, is the amount on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b, less than $61,555 ($68,675 if married filing jointly)? ... No. STOP You can’t take the credit." executable-triage-v2 (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/eic.json`; 1,382 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 161 partial records; 2,043 comparisons): 1,998 of 2,043 agree; 40 candidate-refuses, 3 candidate-unknown, 2 project-refuses; on returns the full interview completes: 1 candidate-unknown. Every completed-return difference was read with the witness cap lifted (triage.mjs run directly; scratch /tmp/adj-fixr2-dependent-eic/eic.full.json): the 1 completed difference is a dependent.us_half unknown on audit.household.hoh_married_no_community_property (line 11b $120,000.00): the reply's Step 1 limit is chosen by its count of qualifying children with valid SSNs (eic.h_limit through eic.h_kids and dependent.eic_ssn_child), which needs each dependent's dependent.us_half even where line 11b is at or above the largest limit and question 1 is No for any count; the project tests that limit first (eic.below_top) and the interview does not ask those answers there. A candidate that needs answers the source does not need is a model error (README). Off completed returns: 2 more such unknowns. The candidate-refuses are its gates on refused returns (dependent.other_claimant 20, sa.excluded_income 12, dependent.married 8): scope. The 2 project-refuses are cited screens on refused returns (f8889 unsupported, retirement special). No value difference: the previous round's married-filing-separately reading is gone. Project stands.

Second transcription's note: The worksheet's Form 8862 caution is covered by the listed screening input.

Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 40, Step 1, question 1: "If, in 2025: 3 or more children who have valid SSNs lived with you, is the amount on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b, less than $61,555 ($68,675 if married filing jointly)? ... No. STOP You can’t take the credit." i1040gi p. 46, Worksheet A: "Use this worksheet only if you answered “No” to Step 5, question 2. Otherwise, use Worksheet B." i1040gi p. 46, Worksheet A, line 6: "This is your earned income credit." executable-triage-v2 (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/eic.json`; 1,382 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 161 partial records; 2,043 comparisons): 1,935 of 2,043 agree; 47 candidate-unknown, 35 candidate-refuses, 25 candidate-shows-skipped, 1 project-refuses; on returns the full interview completes: 13 candidate-unknown, 10 candidate-shows-skipped. Every completed-return difference was read with the witness cap lifted (triage.mjs run directly; scratch /tmp/adj-fixr2-dependent-eic/eic.full.json): (a) 10 candidate-shows-skipped, line 6 figured and shown after a STOP (stage13.eic_spouse_no_ssn $649.00, stage9.eic.no_child.over65, .under25, .abroad, .qc_of_another $308.00, stage9.eic.investment_income $1,369.00, stage16.cleanup.mut.clwb_three_figured $1,294.00, stage9.s8812.three_children.withholding_exceeds.no_eic $2,261.00, audit.eic.filer_without_eic_ssn $3,261.00, generated 439 $2,894.00); the reply's eic.credit is $0.00 on each; (b) 13 candidate-unknown dependent.us_half: the reply's Step 1 limit is chosen by its count of qualifying children with valid SSNs (eic.h_limit through eic.h_kids and dependent.eic_ssn_child), which needs each dependent's dependent.us_half even where line 11b is at or above the largest limit and question 1 is No for any count; the project tests that limit first (eic.below_top) and the interview does not ask those answers there. A candidate that needs answers the source does not need is a model error (README). Refusals are cited gates on refused returns (scope). Project stands.

## Refusal gates listed by the second transcription

Reported, not judged: the project often refuses before a worksheet is used, on the line that chooses it, which a per-line comparison cannot see.

| eic.credit | eic.h_s4_died, household.credit_disallowed, s8812.combat_pay, sa.excluded_income, scope.other_income, scope.other_line16, scope.schedule1, sd.other_forms | eic.h_s4_died |
| eic.check_27c | eic.h_s4_died, s8812.combat_pay, sa.excluded_income, scope.other_income, scope.other_line16, scope.schedule1, sd.other_forms | eic.h_s4_died |
| eic.separated_box | sa.excluded_income, scope.other_income, scope.other_line16, scope.schedule1, sd.other_forms | - |
| eic.clergy | scope.schedule1 | - |
| (Step 5, 2) | scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | scope.schedule1 |
| (Step 5, 3) | scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | - |
| (Step 5, 4) | s8812.combat_pay | - |
| (Step 5, 5) | s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | - |
| 4b | s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | - |
| 6 (eic.wsb_6) | s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | - |

| 2 | s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | - |
| 4 | s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | - |
| 5 | s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | - |
| 6 (eic.line6) | household.credit_disallowed, s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | household.credit_disallowed |

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
```yaml
lines:
  - id: eic.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero amount"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
  - id: eic.h_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Are you filing a joint return for 2025?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
  - id: eic.h_kids
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of qualifying children who have valid SSNs"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: dependent, field: dependent.one, where: {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_ssn_child, {const: true}]}}
    note: "Open: Step 1 says children with valid SSNs who 'lived with you', while Step 5 and the worksheets say qualifying children with valid SSNs. Chose qualifying children for both. A filer between the two limits gets a zero credit either way, because the AGI lookup is zero. The meaning of dependent.eic_ssn_child is inferred from its name; its computation was not supplied."
  - id: eic.h_qc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of children who meet the conditions to be your qualifying child for the EIC, with or without a valid SSN"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: dependent, field: dependent.one, where: {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_child, {const: true}]}}
    note: "The meaning of dependent.eic_child is inferred from its name; its computation was not supplied."
  - id: eic.h_lim0o
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$19,104"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit0.other}
  - id: eic.h_lim0j
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$26,214 if married filing jointly"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit0.joint}
  - id: eic.h_lim1o
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$50,434"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit1.other}
  - id: eic.h_lim1j
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$57,554 if married filing jointly"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit1.joint}
  - id: eic.h_lim2o
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$57,310"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit2.other}
  - id: eic.h_lim2j
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$64,430 if married filing jointly"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit2.joint}
  - id: eic.h_lim3o
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$61,555"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit3.other}
  - id: eic.h_lim3j
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$68,675 if married filing jointly"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit3.joint}
  - id: eic.h_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Income limit for your number of children and filing status (Step 1, question 1; Step 5, question 3; Worksheet B, line 5)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_kids, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}]}
          then: eic.h_lim0o
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_kids, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}]}
          then: eic.h_lim0j
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_kids, {const: 1, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}]}
          then: eic.h_lim1o
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_kids, {const: 1, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}]}
          then: eic.h_lim1j
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_kids, {const: 2, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}]}
          then: eic.h_lim2o
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_kids, {const: 2, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}]}
          then: eic.h_lim2j
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.h_kids, {const: 3, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}]}
          then: eic.h_lim3o
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.h_kids, {const: 3, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}]}
          then: eic.h_lim3j
  - id: eic.h_agi_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 1, question 1: is the amount on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b, less than the limit?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, lt: [f1040.line11b, eic.h_limit]}
  - id: eic.h_step1_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 1: AGI is under the limit, and you (and your spouse if filing a joint return) have an SSN valid for EIC purposes"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_agi_ok, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [household.all_eic_ssn, {const: true}]}]}
    refuses: [sa.excluded_income, scope.other_income]
    note: "Step 1 question 3 (Form 2555) and question 4 (nonresident alien) are not computed; they are covered by the listed screening inputs."
  - id: eic.h_7a
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 7a. If line 7a is a loss, enter -0-."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1040.line7a, take: {const: 0}}
  - id: eic.h_inv
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 2, question 1: Investment income (lines 2a, 2b, 3b and 7a)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line2a, f1040.line2b, f1040.line3b, eic.h_7a]}
  - id: eic.h_step2_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 2, question 2: your investment income is not more than $11,950"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, le: [eic.h_inv, {param: eic.investment_limit}]}
    refuses: [sd.other_forms, scope.schedule1, scope.other_income, scope.other_line16]
    note: "Step 2 question 3 (Form 4797) and question 4 (Schedule E, personal property rental, Form 8814, passive activity) lead to Pub. 596 Worksheet 1, which is not supplied; they are refused. With those excluded, investment income over the limit is a STOP."
  - id: eic.h_passed12
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Steps 1 and 2 passed"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_step1_ok, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_step2_ok, {const: true}]}]}
  - id: eic.h_married_sep
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Are you a married taxpayer whose filing status is married filing separately or head of household?"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}
        - {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "hoh"}]}, {op: test, eq: [household.married, {const: true}]}]}
  - id: eic.h_has_qc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3, question 1: do you have at least one child who meets the conditions to be your qualifying child?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_qc, {const: 1, type: Count}]}
  - id: eic.h_s3_q5_stop
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3, question 5 STOP: married filing separately or head of household, same principal residence for the last 6 months of 2025, and not legally separated and living apart"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_married_sep, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.same_home, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.legally_separated, {const: false}]}
  - id: eic.h_s3_q6_yes
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3, question 6 answered Yes: you could be a qualifying child of another person"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s3_q5_stop, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: true}]}
  - id: eic.h_step3_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3 passed with a qualifying child; go to Step 5"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_s3_q5_stop, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: false}]}]}
  - id: eic.h_s4_died
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4, question 2 reached, and your spouse died in 2025 or you are preparing a return for someone who died in 2025 (see Pub. 596)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_passed12, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_married_sep, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.died_2025, {const: true}]}
  - id: eic.h_age_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of people on the return born after December 31, 1960, and before January 2, 2001"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: person
      field: person.one
      where: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [person.born_before_2001, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [person.born_after_1960, {const: true}]}]}
    note: "Assumes the person collection holds a spouse entry only on a joint return, so this count reads 'you, or your spouse if filing a joint return'."
  - id: eic.h_s4_q12_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4, questions 1 and 2 passed: no qualifying child, not a married taxpayer filing separately or as head of household, and you or your spouse at least age 25 but under age 65"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_married_sep, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, ge: [eic.h_age_count, {const: 1, type: Count}]}
    refuses: [eic.h_s4_died]
  - id: eic.h_s4_home_bad
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4, question 3 answered No: main home not in the United States for more than half of 2025"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_s4_q12_ok, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.us_home, {const: false}]}]}
  - id: eic.h_s4_qc_yes
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4, question 5 answered Yes: you could be a qualifying child of another person"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s4_q12_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.us_home, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: true}]}
  - id: eic.h_step4_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4 passed; go to Step 5"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s4_q12_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.us_home, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [sa.dependent, {const: false}]}]}
    note: "Question 6 is read from sa.dependent, which also asks about a spouse and dual-status aliens. This test is reached only on a non-joint return that is not a married separate or head of household filer, so no spouse is involved, and a dual-status alien cannot take the credit here either. A Yes is treated as the printed STOP (zero credit), not as a refusal on this form."
  - id: eic.h_reach5
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Steps 1 to 4 passed; go to Step 5"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_passed12, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_step3_ok, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_step4_ok, {const: true}]}
  - id: eic.clergy
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 5, question 1: Are you filing Schedule SE because you were a member of the clergy or you had church employee income of $108.28 or more?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [{const: true}, {const: false}]}
    refuses: [scope.schedule1]
    note: "No input in the closed interface answers this question. Clergy income and church employee income are refused through scope.schedule1, so any computed return has the answer No. Encoded as a constant false."
  - id: eic.self_employed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 5, question 2: Were you self-employed at any time in 2025, or are you filing Schedule SE because you were a member of the clergy or you had church employee income, or are you filing Schedule C as a statutory employee?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, any: [{op: test, eq: [s1.has_sc, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.clergy, {const: true}]}]}
    note: "Self-employment is read as having a Schedule C on the return, because other self-employment is outside the tool's scope. The statutory employee case is screened per business by sc.statutory. The meaning of s1.has_sc is inferred from its name."
  - id: eic.step5_line1
    printed: "(Step 5, 1)"
    label: "Enter the amount from Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 1z"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line1z}
  - id: eic.step5_line2
    printed: "(Step 5, 2)"
    label: "Enter the Medicaid waiver payment amounts excluded from income on Schedule 1, line 8s, unless you choose to include these amounts in earned income, in which case enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
    refuses: [scope.other_income, scope.schedule1]
    note: "Medicaid waiver payments and their exclusion are refused by the screening inputs, so only the -0- case is computed."
  - id: eic.step5_line3
    printed: "(Step 5, 3)"
    label: "Subtract line 2 from line 1"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: eic.step5_line1, take: eic.step5_line2}
  - id: eic.step5_line4
    printed: "(Step 5, 4)"
    label: "Enter all of your nontaxable combat pay if you elect to include it in earned income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
    refuses: [s8812.combat_pay]
    note: "Nontaxable combat pay is refused by the screening input, so only the zero case is computed."
  - id: eic.earned_income
    printed: "(Step 5, 5)"
    label: "Add lines 3 and 4. This is your earned income"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [eic.step5_line3, eic.step5_line4]}
  - id: eic.h_wsb_se
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B, lines 1e, 2c and 3 combined, including your spouse's amounts on a joint return"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: person, field: person.eic_se}
    refuses: [scope.schedule1]
    note: "person.eic_se is read as each person's Part 1 line 1e if filing Schedule SE, otherwise Part 2 line 2b; its computation was not supplied. Farm and Schedule K-1 amounts are refused through scope.schedule1, and Part 3 statutory employee income is screened by sc.statutory, so those amounts are not computed."
  - id: eic.wsb_4b
    printed: "4b"
    label: "Combine lines 1e, 2c, 3, and 4a. This is your total earned income."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [eic.h_wsb_se, eic.earned_income]}
  - id: eic.wsb_6
    printed: "6 (eic.wsb_6)"
    label: "Enter your total earned income from Part 4, line 4b."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: eic.wsb_4b, take: {const: 0}}
    note: "If line 4b is zero or less the worksheet stops before line 6, so the zero floor affects only the displayed value."
  - id: eic.h_earned_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 5, question 3 (no self-employment) or Worksheet B, lines 4b and 5 (self-employment): earned income is under the limit, and Worksheet B line 4b is more than zero"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.earned_income, eic.h_limit]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, gt: [eic.wsb_4b, {const: 0}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.wsb_4b, eic.h_limit]}
  - id: eic.h_eligible
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "All steps passed; figure the credit on the worksheet"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_reach5, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_earned_ok, {const: true}]}]}
    note: "Step 6 (having the IRS figure the credit) has no input in the closed interface; the credit is always figured on the worksheet."
  - id: eic.line1
    printed: "1"
    label: "Enter your earned income from Step 5."
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: false}]}
          then: eic.earned_income
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.wsb_6
    note: "Worksheet B lines 7 to 11 repeat Worksheet A lines 2 to 6 with the same tables and thresholds, and were not requested as separate lines. Chose to carry both worksheets on lines 1 to 6: for a Worksheet B filer, line 1 holds Worksheet B line 6, and lines 2 to 6 hold Worksheet B lines 7 to 11."
  - id: eic.h_in_table
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "The amount on line 1 is within the EIC Table for your column (at least $1 and under the limit)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, ge: [eic.line1, {param: eic.table_start}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [eic.line1, eic.h_limit]}
  - id: eic.h_t2_0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 1, no qualifying children"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_0, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_t2_1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 1, one qualifying child"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_1, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_t2_2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 1, two qualifying children"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_2, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_t2_3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 1, three or more qualifying children"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_3, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.line2
    printed: "2"
    label: "Look up the amount on line 1 above in the EIC Table to find the credit. Be sure you use the correct column for your filing status and the number of qualifying children you have who have valid SSNs as defined earlier. Enter the credit here."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_in_table, {const: false}]}
          then: eic.h_zero
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_in_table, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_kids, {const: 0, type: Count}]}]}
          then: eic.h_t2_0
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_in_table, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_kids, {const: 1, type: Count}]}]}
          then: eic.h_t2_1
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_in_table, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_kids, {const: 2, type: Count}]}]}
          then: eic.h_t2_2
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_in_table, {const: true}]}, {op: test, ge: [eic.h_kids, {const: 3, type: Count}]}]}
          then: eic.h_t2_3
    note: "Open: the printed table starts at $1 and does not state a result for an amount under $1. Chose zero for that case."
  - id: eic.line3
    printed: "3"
    label: "Enter the amount from Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line11b}
  - id: eic.line4
    printed: "4"
    label: "Are the amounts on lines 3 and 1 the same?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [eic.line3, eic.line1]}
  - id: eic.h_th0o
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$10,620"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold0.other}
  - id: eic.h_th0j
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$17,730 if married filing jointly"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold0.joint}
  - id: eic.h_th1o
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$23,350"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold1.other}
  - id: eic.h_th1j
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$30,470 if married filing jointly"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold1.joint}
  - id: eic.h_threshold
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 5 threshold for your number of qualifying children and filing status"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_kids, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}]}
          then: eic.h_th0o
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_kids, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}]}
          then: eic.h_th0j
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.h_kids, {const: 1, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}]}
          then: eic.h_th1o
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.h_kids, {const: 1, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}]}
          then: eic.h_th1j
  - id: eic.h_use5
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 4 is No and the amount on line 3 is not less than the threshold, so look up line 3 and enter the smaller of lines 5 and 2 on line 6"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.line4, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, ge: [eic.line3, eic.h_threshold]}
  - id: eic.h_lookup5
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 3 is looked up and is within the EIC Table for your column"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_use5, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [eic.line3, eic.h_limit]}
  - id: eic.h_t5_0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 3, no qualifying children"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_0, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_t5_1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 3, one qualifying child"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_1, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_t5_2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 3, two qualifying children"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_2, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_t5_3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table credit for line 3, three or more qualifying children"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_3, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.line5
    printed: "5"
    label: "If the amount on line 3 is not less than the threshold, look up the amount on line 3 in the EIC Table to find the credit. Be sure you use the correct column for your filing status and the number of qualifying children you have who have valid SSNs. Enter the credit here."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_lookup5, {const: false}]}
          then: eic.h_zero
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_lookup5, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_kids, {const: 0, type: Count}]}]}
          then: eic.h_t5_0
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_lookup5, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_kids, {const: 1, type: Count}]}]}
          then: eic.h_t5_1
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_lookup5, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_kids, {const: 2, type: Count}]}]}
          then: eic.h_t5_2
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_lookup5, {const: true}]}, {op: test, ge: [eic.h_kids, {const: 3, type: Count}]}]}
          then: eic.h_t5_3
    note: "A line 5 the IRS leaves blank is represented as zero; line 6 does not read it in that case (eic.h_use5). An amount at or above the limit is also zero, because Step 1 stops that filer before the worksheet."
  - id: eic.h_smaller
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Look at the amounts on lines 5 and 2. Then, enter the smaller amount on line 6."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [eic.line5, eic.line2]}
  - id: eic.line6
    printed: "6 (eic.line6)"
    label: "This is your earned income credit."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_use5, {const: false}]}
          then: eic.line2
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_use5, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.h_smaller
    refuses: [household.credit_disallowed]
    note: "The worksheet's Form 8862 caution is covered by the listed screening input."
  - id: eic.credit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Earned income credit to enter on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 27a; zero if any step says you can't take the credit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_eligible, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.line6
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_eligible, {const: false}]}
          then: eic.h_zero
  - id: eic.check_27c
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Check the box on line 27c if you have been instructed to check the box in the instructions for line 27a"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_agi_ok, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [household.all_eic_ssn, {const: false}]}]}
        - {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_passed12, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s3_q6_yes, {const: true}]}]}
        - {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_passed12, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s4_home_bad, {const: true}]}]}
        - {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_passed12, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s4_qc_yes, {const: true}]}]}
        - {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_reach5, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_earned_ok, {const: false}]}]}
        - {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_eligible, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.line2, {const: 0}]}]}
    note: "Only STOPs that print the line 27c instruction set this box. The other STOPs (for example Step 1 question 1, Step 2, and Step 5 question 3) do not. The election 'if you do not want to claim the earned income credit' has no input in the closed interface and is not represented. The nonresident alien instruction is covered by the scope.other_income refusal."
  - id: eic.separated_box
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Check the box in the Dependents section that discusses the special rule for separated spouses"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_passed12, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_married_sep, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s3_q5_stop, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: false}]}
    note: "Open: read literally, the No answer to Step 3 question 6 tells every filer reaching it to check this box, including unmarried filers. Chose married taxpayers filing separately or as head of household only, because the Special rule for separated spouses applies only 'If you are married, but not filing a joint return'."
```
```
